1 Executive Summary
188
1.1 Target
189
Thisisasearchforleptoquarksignalsinthe𝜏𝜈𝑏 finalstate,motivatedbythe 𝑅(𝐷(∗)) anomaliesbythe
190
BaBar, Belle and LHCb experiments. It is a completely new phase space, previously not explored by
191
ATLAS and CMS1. Since the signal strength scales with the 4th power of the coupling constant, the
192
sensitivitygainfromincreasedintegratedluminosityisratherlimited(ofcoursethelimitonthesignal
193
cross-sectionwillbeimprovedbyaddingmoredata,though). WeagreedwithPC/PubComandrelevant
194
convenerstousetheRun-2datasetonlyandaimforanearlypublicationbytheendof2025.
195
1.2 Context and Motivation
196
Motivatethisanalysisin1paragraph: whyisthissignatureinteresting? Whichkindof
197
modelsareyouprobing?
198
While the 𝑅(𝐷(∗)) anomaly requires both 𝑏𝜏 and 𝑐𝜈 couplings of the leptoquark, previous ATLAS
199
and CMS searches, focusing on the 𝜏𝜏 final state, were sensitive only to the 𝑏𝜏 coupling and typically
200
fixedthe𝑐𝜈 couplingtoasmallvalue. Thisassumptionwasmotivatednotbyexperimentalconstraints
201
but by theoretical considerations: a general UV completion to suppress Δ𝐹 = 2 transitions. A more
202
model-independentapproachiscrucial,astheUVmodelmayincludemechanismsthatnaturallysuppress
203
flavortransitions,suchastheGIMmechanismintheSM.Oursearchinthe𝜏𝜈𝑏 finalstateissensitiveto
204
both𝑏𝜏 and𝑐𝜈 couplingssimultaneously,andthusuniquelyprobesscenarioswithlarger𝑐𝜈 couplingsthat
205
werepreviouslyunexplored.
206
Howistheanalysisdonein1paragraph: whatarethemainBGprocessesandhowdoyou
207
estimatethem(aretheyMC-ordata-driven,whatisthegeneralideaofthecontrolregions,
208
...),generalcharacteristicsofthePLfit(whichdistribution,binned?,...)
209
Wewillperformasimplecut-and-countanalysiswithtwosingle-binSRs. Eventswithhigh-𝑝 hadronic𝜏
210 T
and high-𝐸miss are selected. Two SRs are categorized by the 𝑏-jet 𝑝 : one with a high-𝑝 𝑏-jet for an
211 T T T
on-shellleptoquarksignaldecayingto𝑏𝜏;andtheotherwithlow-𝑝 𝑏-jetforanon-resonant𝜏𝜈 signalvia
212 T
𝑡-channelexchangeoftheleptoquark. ThemainSMbackgroundis (𝑊 → 𝜏𝜈)+jets. Top-quarkproduction
213
(𝑡𝑡¯and single-𝑡) is subdominant. For these components, dedicated CRs are prepared and involved in
214
1Therewasa𝑊+𝑏measurementsinthepast,butitemployed𝑒and𝜇channelsonly[1]. ThereweresearchesforSUSYor
Leptoquarksignalsin𝑏,𝜏and𝐸miss finalstates,buttheyrequiredmultiple𝑏or𝜏[2–4],butwecouldn’tfindsearchesin
T
ATLASwithasingle𝜏,single𝑏-jetandlarge𝐸miss.
T

ATLASDRAFT
the likelihood to give a constraint from data to the MC simulation. WCR is defined by replacing the
215
𝜏 in the SR with an electron or muon. TopCR requires an additional electron or muon to the SR. The
216
backgroundestimationstrategyischeckedcarefullyintheVRs. TheSMbackgroundwithfake𝜏 lepton
217
(mostly from (𝑍 → 𝜈𝜈)+jets) is expected to have very small yields, and will be estimated by the MC
218
simulationdirectly,afteracarefulcheckin (𝑍 →ℓℓ)+fake-𝜏VR.Forus,thefake𝜏rejectionisnotamajor
219
concern,andthesensitivitydependsmostlyonthesignalefficiency. WewillthereforeusetheRNNTau
220
identification,withoutwaitingforthelatestrecommendationfortheGNTau,forwhichwealreadyhavea
221
CPrecommendationfortheRun-2dataset.

2 Introduction
231
A test of the lepton flavor universality (LFU) has been made via the ratio of the branching fractions
232
𝑅(𝐷(∗)) = B(𝐵 → 𝐷(∗)𝜏𝜈)/B(𝐵 → 𝐷(∗)ℓ𝜈) by the BaBar[5, 6], Belle[7–10], Belle-II[11, 12] and
233
LHCb[13–16]experiments,whereℓ = 𝑒,𝜇(ℓ = 𝜇forLHCb). Thelatestaverageofthesemeasurements
234
calculatedbytheHFLAVcollaboration[17]isshowninFigure2.1. Itreports 𝑅(𝐷) and 𝑅(𝐷∗) exceedthe
235
StandardModel(SM)predictions[18–27]by2.5𝜎 and2.3𝜎 respectively. Consideringthe 𝑅(𝐷)-𝑅(𝐷∗)
236
correlation of −0.374, the resulting combined 𝜒2 is 17.63 for 2 degree of freedom, corresponding to a
237
𝑝-valueof1.48×10−4. ThedifferencewiththeSMpredictionscorrespondstoabout3.8𝜎. Thiscanbea
238
hintofanewphysicsbeyondtheSM,anditisimportantforBelle-IIandLHCbtofollowitupwithmore
239
data.

Figure 2.1: Average of 𝑅(𝐷∗) and 𝑅(𝐷)[17] measured by the BaBar[5, 6], Belle[7–10], Belle-II[11, 12], and
LHCb[14–16]experiments.
Apossiblesolutiontoexplainthe𝑅(𝐷(∗))anomalyistointroduceahypotheticalnewparticle,aleptoquark,
241
whichcouplestobothleptonsandquarks,asshowninFig.2.2. Acomprehensiveprogramtosearchfor
242
leptoquarksignalswith𝜏leptonsinthefinalstatehasbeenconductedbytheATLASandCMSexperiments,
243
forbothscalarandvectorleptoquarkscenarios[28–31]. Assumingtheleptoquarkpairproduction,thecross

onGitLab.

7 Background Estimation Strategy
546
ThemainsourceoftheSMbackgroundinthisanalysisis (𝑊 → 𝜏𝜈)+jets,andtop-quarkevents(𝑡𝑡¯and
547
𝑊𝑡)aresubdominant. Forthesetwocomponents,freenormalizationfactorsareintroduced,asdescribedin
548
Section10. DedicatedCRsaredefinedtodeterminethenormalizationfactorsandareinvolvedinthefit.
549
Thedata/MCcomparisoninCRsisdiscussedinSection8.
550
Eventswithfake𝜏 lepton(mainly (𝑍 → 𝜈𝜈)+jetsforlarge 𝐸miss)areminorsourceoftheSMbackground
551 T
for this analysis. They are estimated by the simulation directly. Modeling of 𝑗 → 𝜏 fake rate in the
552
simulation is validated in (𝑍 → ℓℓ)+fake-𝜏 VR in Appendix D. Multi-jet events with both fake 𝜏 and
553
fake 𝐸miss arefoundtobenegligibleaftertheQCDcleaningcutsinAppendixC.Theotherbackground
554 T
(diboson,includingreal𝜏,forwhichthemodelingisrelativelyreliable)isalsoestimatedbythesimulation,
555
withconservativeuncertaintiesonthecrosssections.
556

9 Systematic Uncertainties
685
9.1 Experimental sources
686
Listofexperimentalsourcesofsystematicuncertaintyconsideredinthisanalysisisshowninnextpages.
687
Symmetrization of (up-down)/2 or (abs(up)+abs(min))/2 is performed for most systematics due to the
688
limitedstatisticsofthebackgroundinSR,exceptforJER,mET,andttbarNNLOuncertainties.
689
9.1.1 Luminosityandpileupreweighting
690
The uncertainty in the combined 2015–2018 integrated luminosity is 0.83% [41], obtained using the
691
LUCID-2detector[42]fortheprimaryluminositymeasurements,complementedbymeasurementsusing
692
theinnerdetectorandcalorimeters.
693
TheuncertaintyassociatedtothepileupreweightingisfollowedbyprescriptioninRef.[43]. Thescale
694
factorisappliedtothedatasample,toadjustthe 𝜇 valueforbetterdata/MCagreementonthenumberof
695
verticesdistributionafterthereweighting. Thenominalfactoris1/1.03,withthevariationfrom1/1.07to
696
1/0.99.
697
9.1.2 Tauenergyscaleandefficiencycalibrationuncertainties
698
Thelatest2025prerecommendationisusedwithAnalysisBase,25.2.51.
699
Tauenergyscale(TES) Thein-situmeasurementsusing 𝑍 → 𝜏𝜏 events(INSITUEXP,FIT)covering
700
fromlow-𝑝 regionwereperformed. Uncertaintiesathigh-𝑝 (DETECTOR,PHYSICSLIST,etc.)
701 T T
correspond to old uncertainties derived from test beam and MC comparison, which should still
702
beusedtoo. TheTES andrelateduncertainties areprovided throughtheTauSmearingToolwith
703
“2025-prerec”and“mc20”configurations. Thecorrectionislessthan1%withabsoluteuncertainty
704
oflessthan1.5%.
705
TauefficiencySFs Uncertaintiesonthescalefactorsfortaureconstruction,RNN-basedidentificationand
706
eVetoareconsidered. TauEfficiencyCorrectionToolisconfiguredwith“2025-prerec”and“mc20”
707
options. TaureconstructionefficiencyuncertaintyisTAUS_TRUEHADTAU_EFF_RECO_TOTAL.
708
TauIDefficiencieswereestimatedfor1-prongand3-prongtausseparately. UncorrelatedNPsare
709
assigned for different 𝑝 bins. Central value of the SFs is around 1 in all 𝑝 bins, with 5–8%
710 T T
(1-prong)and6–11%(3-prong)uncertaintiesfortheTightIDworkingpoint.
711
60

9.1.3 Jetand 𝑬miss uncertainties
712 T
TheR22“Phase-1”pre-recommendationisusedforJES/JER,pileupsubtractionand 𝐸miss uncertainties.
713 T
CategoryReductionscheme(about30+5NPs)isusedforJESuncertainties,whileFullJER(13+2NPs)
714
schemeforJER.
715
MC-based steps of the calibration were derived using R22 MC20 FullSimulation samples. Extra
716
uncertaintiesareaddedtotakeintoaccountdifferencesbetweenR21andR22(updatedtopoclusternoise
717
thresholdsandtracking/vertexingchanges):
718
• JESUnc_VertexingAlg_PreRec
719
• JESUnc_Noise_PreRec
720
Forin-situcalibration,R21studiesareusedwithextrauncertaintiestocorrectfornon-closurewhenthe
721
R21in-situcalibrationsareappliedtoR22samples:
722
• EtaIntercalibration_NonClosure_PreRec
723
• InSitu_NonClosure_PreRec
724
• JERUnc_Noise_PreRec
725
OnlyforAF3(signal)samples: extrauncertaintycomingfromjetresponseandresolutionnon-closure
726
betweenFullSimandAF3isapplied.
727
• RelativeNonClosure_AF3
728
• JERUnc_AF3vsFullSim_AF3_PreRec
729
WedoNOTusetheMC-to-MCcalibrationoptionforJESflavorresponseuncertainty. WedoNOTfollow
730
the JES precision reduced flavor uncertainties prescription, so the Flavor_Response and Composition
731
uncertaintiesarecalculatedwiththedefaultconservativeoption.
732
TheJERuncertaintiessmearboth(pseudo-)dataandMCevents[44]. ShiftingagivenNPby+1𝜎 will
733
correspond with smearing either the (pseudo-)data or MC, while shifting the same NP by −1𝜎 will
734
correspondwithsmearingtheMCor(pseudo-)data. Iftheuncertaintyforagivencomponentcrosseszero,
735
thenthiswillchangewhether(pseudo)-dataorMCissmeared,thusproperlyretainingtheanti-correlated
736
behavior implied by the uncertainty, rather than enforcing a correlated behavior. Some more de-
737
tailsinhttps://twiki.cern.ch/twiki/bin/view/AtlasProtected/JetUncertaintiesRel22#
738
Different_smearing_options.

tion:https://ftag.docs.cern.ch/recommendations/. TheMediumeigenvectorreductionscheme

755

759

760
50%uncertainty,thecontributionsfrom𝑊+jetsandTopbackgroundsinTopCRsandWCRsaremarginal.

1028

tothisanalysis. Theinterferenceeffectistakenintoaccountinthefit,bynormalizingtheinterferenceterm

1077

1083

1084
8intheauxiliarymaterial.

1086

1087

13 Conclusion
1093
A first search for high-mass leptoquark signals with ATLAS is presented, targeting final states with a
1094
hadronically-decaying𝜏 lepton,a𝑏-jetandmissingtransversemomentum. Twosignalregionsaredefined:
1095
oneforthe“resonance”signals,correspondingtoasingleproductionoftheleptoquarkdecayingto 𝑏𝜏 in
1096
associationwithaneutrino,andtheotherforthe“non-resonance”ones,consideringthe𝑡-channelexchange
1097
oftheleptoquark,whichmanifestsasanexcessinthehigh-𝑚 tailof 𝜏 and 𝐸miss. Afterthehigh-𝑝 𝜏
1098 T T T
and 𝐸miss requirements,thefaketaucontributionisstronglysuppressed,andthemainsourceoftheSM
1099 T
backgroundis (𝑊 → 𝜏𝜈)+jetsandtop-quarkeventscontainingreal𝜏 leptons. Dedicatedcontrolregions
1100
are defined for these backgrounds, and included in a profile-likelihood fit to give a constraint on their
1101
normalizations.
1102
In resonance and non-resonance SRs, 3 and 2 events are observed with the background expectations
1103
2.69±0.71and1.88±0.65. GiventhatnosignificantexcessisobservedinbothSRs,theupperlimits
1104
1105 onthe𝑈 1 vectorleptoquarkcouplingsaresetat95%CL.Forthepureleft-handedscenariowith 𝛽3 𝑅 3 = 0,
1106 theobserved(expected)upperlimitson 𝑔 𝑈 are0.71(0.68) at 𝛽2 𝐿 3 = 2.0and2.84(2.81) at 𝛽2 𝐿 3 = 0.2for
leptoquarkmassof1.5TeV,and1.42(1.40) at 𝛽23 = 2.0and4.83(4.78) at 𝛽23 = 0.2forleptoquarkmass
1107 𝐿 𝐿
of2.5TeV. Theresultstestedpartoftheregioncompatiblewiththe 𝑅(𝐷(∗)) anomalyat 𝛽23 > 1.8.

A Signal Validations
1262
A.1 Test samples for the initial study
1263
Two mass points of 1.5 TeV and 2.5 TeV were generated for the initial studies, using configurations
1264
e8531(rel23.6.10),andDAODsarelistedinTableA.2. The1.5TeVsampleswereprocessedwithboth
1265
Geant4(s3767)andAF3(a907)butthe2.5TeVwasonlywithAF3. Intheeventgeneration,parametersof
1266
1267 thematricesinEq.4.1weresetto𝑔 𝑈 = 1and 𝛽2 𝐿 3 = 0.2. The 𝛽3 𝑅 3 wassetto0. Thesettingparametersare
summarizedinTableA.1. Thetauisspecifiedtobeonlyhadronicallydecayedtoenhancethesignalevents.
1268
TheBSMeventswerefurtherseparatedbyBFilter/BVetofilterswhichcheckedthe𝑏-quarkexistenceafter
1269
1270
Pythia: when𝑏-quarkswith |𝜂| <5(any 𝑝
T
)arefound,eventsarefiltered/rejected,respectively. Forthe
interferenceevents,thesumofneutrino’smomentaintheME(MadGraph),whichiscallednet,wasused
1271
sincewefoundadestructiveeffectoftheinterferencetermwasmitigatedathigh-𝐸miss region. Weused
1272 T
net> 150GeV/< 150GeV,whichcorrespondedtomissetandmissetmaxintheMadGraph. Notethat
1273
thesevariablesdon’tincludethemomentaofneutrinosfromtau-decay,whichisperformedinthePythiastep.
1274
1275 Onlyat 𝑀 LQ = 1.5TeV,theadditional“inclusive”samplesnotseparatedintoBSMandInterferencewere
generatedforthevalidation. 10Meventsweregeneratedforeachsample. Thereareinternaleventweights
1276
tovary𝑔 , 𝛽23 and 𝑀 parameters,sothatwecanscanthemintheinterpretation. Therangesof𝑔 , 𝛽23,
1277 𝑈 𝐿 LQ 𝑈 𝐿
1278
and 𝑀
LQ
are {0.5,1,1.5,2.5,3.5}, {0,0.2,0.6,1,1.4}, and {1.5,2,2.5,3,4,5} (TeV), respectively. The
othercouplingsweresetto0sincetheyareirrelevanttooursignal(the 𝜅 parameterisalsosetto0,sofor
1279
nowwecantest𝑈Yang-Mills modelonly).
1280 1

1421

1425

1426

1427

onjet 𝑝 distributionbetweene-andmu-channelsareperformed,andshowninFigureF.1. Thee-and
1429 T

1430

1433

1434 T

1435 T

1436 T
theyarenotfoundinelectronchannel,butthereisoneeventinmuonchannelat 𝑝 > 50GeV. Thismakes
1437 T
theMCyieldsinthemuonchannelhigherthantheelectronchannel.
